bias <- colMeans(cohort3$t) - cohort3$t0
se <- apply(cohort3$t, 2, sd)

df.coh3 <- data.frame(original=original, bias=bias, se=se)

row.names(df.coh3) <- c("AME_gender_m1", "AME_gender_m2", "AME_gender_m3a", "AME_gender_m3b", "AME_gender_m3c", "AME_gender_m4", "AME_time_m1_0", "AME_time_m1_1", "AME_time_m1_2", "AME_time_m1_3", "AME_time_m1_4", "AME_time_m1_5", "AME_time_m2_0", "AME_time_m2_1", "AME_time_m2_2", "AME_time_m2_3", "AME_time_m2_4", "AME_time_m2_5", "AME_time_m3a_0", "AME_time_m3a_1", "AME_time_m3a_2", "AME_time_m3a_3", "AME_time_m3a_4", "AME_time_m3a_5", "AME_time_m3b_0", "AME_time_m3b_1", "AME_time_m3b_2", "AME_time_m3b_3", "AME_time_m3b_4", "AME_time_m3b_5", "AME_time_m3c_0", "AME_time_m3c_1", "AME_time_m3c_2", "AME_time_m3c_3", "AME_time_m3c_4", "AME_time_m3c_5", "AME_time_m4_0", "AME_time_m4_1", "AME_time_m4_2", "AME_time_m4_3", "AME_time_m4_4", "AME_time_m4_5","AME_cpubs_m3a", "AME_cpubs_m3b", "AME_cpubs_m3c", "AME_cpubs_m4", "AME_ppubs_m3a", "AME_ppubs_m3b", "AME_ppubs_m3c", "AME_ppubs_m4", "AME_netsize_m3b", "AME_netsize_m3c", "AME_netsize_m4", "AME_prof_m3b", "AME_prof_m3c", "AME_prof_m4", "AME_svpubs_m3b", "AME_svpubs_m3c", "AME_svpubs_m4", "AME_womsv_m3c", "AME_womsv_m4", "AME_genderwomsv_m4")

# Creating neat labels
table_ames_coh1 <- df.coh1 %>%
  mutate(term = rownames(df.coh1),
         var = str_extract(term, "(?<=AME_)[^_]+"),
         model = str_extract(term, "m[1-4][abc]?")) %>%
  mutate(model = recode(model,
                        "m1" = "M1",
                        "m2" = "M2",
                        "m3a" = "M3",
                        "m3b" = "M4",
                        "m3c" = "M5",
                        "m4" = "M6"),
         model = factor(model, levels = paste0("M", 1:6))) %>%
  mutate(label = case_when(
    str_detect(term, "gendercohort") ~ "PhD cohort * Woman",
    str_detect(term, "genderwomsv") ~ "Women supervisors * Woman",
    str_detect(term, "gender") ~ "Woman",
    str_detect(term, "time") ~ paste0("Time since PhD: ", str_extract(term, "[:digit:]$"), " years"),
    str_detect(term, "cohort") ~ "PhD cohort",
    str_detect(term, "womsv") ~ "Women supervisors",
    str_detect(term, "cpubs") ~ "Cumulative publications",
    str_detect(term, "ppubs") ~ "Previous publications",
    str_detect(term, "netsize") ~ "Team size",
    str_detect(term, "prof") ~ "2+ professors",
    str_detect(term, "svpubs") ~ "Supervisors' productivity",
    TRUE ~ str_to_title(var)
  )) %>%
  mutate(value = sprintf("%.4f (%.4f)", original, se)) %>%
  select(label, model, value)

# correct table ordering
rows_tab <- c("Woman", "Time since PhD: 1 years", "Time since PhD: 2 years", "Time since PhD: 3 years", "Time since PhD: 4 years", "Time since PhD: 5 years", "Time since PhD: 6 years", "Time since PhD: 7 years", "Time since PhD: 8 years", "PhD cohort", "PhD cohort * Woman", "Cumulative publications", "Previous publications", "Team size", "2+ professors", "Supervisors' productivity", "Women supervisors", "Women supervisors * Woman")


# Reshape to wide format

Cohort 2 (2007-2014)

# Creating neat labels
table_ames_coh2 <- df.coh2 %>%
  mutate(term = rownames(df.coh2),
         var = str_extract(term, "(?<=AME_)[^_]+"),
         model = str_extract(term, "m[1-4][abc]?")) %>%
  mutate(model = recode(model,
                        "m1" = "M1",
                        "m2" = "M2",
                        "m3a" = "M3",
                        "m3b" = "M4",
                        "m3c" = "M5",
                        "m4" = "M6"),
         model = factor(model, levels = paste0("M", 1:6))) %>%
  mutate(label = case_when(
    str_detect(term, "gendercohort") ~ "PhD cohort * Woman",
    str_detect(term, "genderwomsv") ~ "Women supervisors * Woman",
    str_detect(term, "gender") ~ "Woman",
    str_detect(term, "time") ~ paste0("Time since PhD: ", str_extract(term, "[:digit:]$"), " years"),
    str_detect(term, "cohort") ~ "PhD cohort",
    str_detect(term, "womsv") ~ "Women supervisors",
    str_detect(term, "cpubs") ~ "Cumulative publications",
    str_detect(term, "ppubs") ~ "Previous publications",
    str_detect(term, "netsize") ~ "Team size",
    str_detect(term, "prof") ~ "2+ professors",
    str_detect(term, "svpubs") ~ "Supervisors' productivity",
    TRUE ~ str_to_title(var)
  )) %>%
  mutate(value = sprintf("%.4f (%.4f)", original, se)) %>%
  select(label, model, value)

# correct table ordering
rows_tab <- c("Woman", "Time since PhD: 1 years", "Time since PhD: 2 years", "Time since PhD: 3 years", "Time since PhD: 4 years", "Time since PhD: 5 years", "Time since PhD: 6 years", "Time since PhD: 7 years", "Time since PhD: 8 years", "PhD cohort", "PhD cohort * Woman", "Cumulative publications", "Previous publications", "Team size", "2+ professors", "Supervisors' productivity", "Women supervisors", "Women supervisors * Woman")

  pivot_wider(names_from = model, values_from = value) %>%
  distinct() %>%
  mutate(label = factor(label, levels=rows_tab)) %>%
  arrange(label)
